- **md-to-linkedin**: Convert a Markdown article into rich text for LinkedIn's article editor, which has no Markdown and renders no tables. Headings, bold, italics, lists, quotes, links and fenced code become real HTML; tables become bullet lists, because the editor cannot draw one, or screenshot to PNG when the grid matters. Use when the user wants to publish a markdown draft as a LinkedIn article, asks for LinkedIn rich text, or needs the article editor formatting preserved. - **md-to-medium**: Convert a Markdown article into an HTML fragment Medium's story editor accepts on paste. Medium deletes any image whose source is a data: URI, silently, so this converter never inlines bytes: every image is pointed at a public URL and the script refuses to pass until those images are pushed. Tables become hosted PNGs, because Medium draws no table. The article's H1 is dropped for the Title field. Use when the user wants to publish a markdown draft to Medium, asks for Medium rich text, or reports that a LinkedIn payload pasted into Medium lost its images. ([j3ffyang/ai-thoughts](https://github.com/j3ffyang/ai-thoughts/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/md-to-medium) · ★1)
